  haccp_plan_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to foodsafety.haccp_plan. Business justification: stock_transfer has explicit \'haccp_monitoring_required_flag\' and \'temperature_controlled_flag\' fields. When HACCP monitoring is required during inter-unit transfers, the governing HACCP plan must be r']
  origin_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the restaurant unit or distribution center from which inventory is being transferred. For intra-unit transfers, this is the same as destination unit.']
  origin_stock_location_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific storage location (walk-in cooler, dry storage, freezer) within the origin unit from which items are being transferred. Critical for BOH (Back of House) inventory accuracy.']
  primary_stock_requested_by_employee_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the employee (typically kitchen manager or shift supervisor) who initiated the stock transfer request. Used for accountability and audit trail.']
  shift_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.shift. Business justification: Inter-location stock transfers occur during specific shifts. Shift-level labor attribution for transfer activities and shift productivity reports are standard in multi-unit restaurant operations. No e']
  tertiary_stock_received_by_employee_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the employee at the destination unit who physically received and confirmed the transferred inventory. Null until transfer is received.']
  cancellation_date date [note: 'Date when the stock transfer was cancelled. Null if transfer was not cancelled.']
  cancellation_reason varchar [note: 'Free-text explanation for why the transfer was cancelled. Used for operational analysis and process improvement.']
  carrier_name varchar [note: 'Name of the transportation carrier or delivery service used for inter-unit transfers. Null for intra-unit transfers or self-pickup.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this stock transfer record was first created in the system. Used for audit trail and data lineage tracking.']
  expected_delivery_date date [note: 'Planned or estimated date when the transferred inventory is expected to arrive at the destination. Used for receiving planning and PAR (Periodic Automatic Replenishment) level management.']
  external_transfer_reference varchar [note: 'Unique identifier from the source operational system (MarketMan, SAP MM). Used for data lineage, reconciliation, and cross-system traceability.']
  fiscal_period varchar [note: 'Fiscal period (year and period number) to which this transfer is assigned for financial reporting. Format: YYYY-PNN (e.g., 2024-P03 for period 3 of fiscal year 2024).']
  gl_posting_date date [note: 'Date when the inventory transfer transaction was posted to the general ledger for financial reporting. May differ from physical transfer date due to period-end cutoffs.']
  haccp_monitoring_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this transfer requires continuous temperature monitoring and documentation per HACCP critical control point protocols. True for high-risk perishable items.']
  inspection_notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes from quality inspection documenting any issues, observations, or conditions. Used for quality tracking and supplier performance evaluation.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this stock transfer record was most recently updated. Used for change tracking and data synchronization.']
  priority_level varchar [note: 'Urgency classification for the transfer: routine (standard replenishment), high (needed within 24 hours), urgent (needed same day), emergency (critical stockout situation requiring immediate action).']
  quality_inspection_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether items in this transfer require formal quality inspection upon receipt per GMP (Good Manufacturing Practice) or food safety protocols. True for high-value or high-risk items.']
  quality_inspection_status varchar [note: 'Result of quality inspection at receiving: not-required (no inspection needed), pending (awaiting inspection), passed (accepted), failed (rejected), conditional-accept (accepted with notes or partial rejection).']
  shipping_method varchar [note: 'Method used to transport inventory between locations: internal-delivery (company fleet), courier (contracted delivery service), third-party (3PD logistics provider), self-pickup (destination unit retrieves), direct-transfer (hand-carried for intra-unit).']
  source_system_code varchar [note: 'Code identifying the operational system that originated this stock transfer record: MARKETMAN (MarketMan Inventory Management), SAP-MM (SAP Materials Management), MANUAL (manually entered), LEGACY (migrated from prior system).']
  temperature_controlled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this transfer requires temperature-controlled transportation to maintain food safety and quality. True for perishable items requiring refrigeration or freezing per HACCP (Hazard Analysis Critical Control Points) standards.']
  temperature_zone_required varchar [note: 'Required temperature zone for transport: ambient (room temperature), refrigerated (33-40°F), frozen (0°F or below), multi-temp (mixed temperature requirements). Critical for HACCP compliance and food safety.']
  total_item_count varchar [note: 'Total number of distinct SKU (Stock Keeping Unit) line items included in this transfer. Used for transfer complexity assessment and receiving workload planning.']
  total_quantity_transferred decimal(12,3) [note: 'Aggregate quantity of all items transferred, summed across all line items. Unit of measure varies by item (cases, pounds, gallons, each). Used for high-level transfer volume tracking.']
  total_transfer_value_usd decimal(15,2) [note: 'Total dollar value of all items included in this stock transfer, calculated at standard cost. Critical for COGS% (Cost of Goods Sold Percentage) tracking and inventory valuation across units.']
  tracking_number varchar [note: 'Shipment tracking number provided by carrier for monitoring in-transit status. Null for internal transfers without formal tracking.']
  transfer_approval_date date [note: 'Date when the stock transfer was approved by authorized manager. Null if not yet approved or if auto-approved (same as request date).']
  transfer_number varchar [note: 'Business-facing unique transfer document number used for tracking and reference in operational systems and communications. Format: STR-YYYYMMDD-NNNN.']
  transfer_reason_code varchar [note: 'Business reason for initiating the stock transfer: par-replenishment (restore PAR levels), excess-stock (reduce overstock), expiring-soon (move near-expiry items), quality-issue (return defective goods), menu-change (redistribute due to menu update), seasonal-adjustment (rebalance for seasonal demand).']
  transfer_reason_notes varchar [note: 'Free-text explanation providing additional context for the transfer. Used for operational communication and audit documentation.']
  transfer_received_date date [note: 'Date when the inventory was physically received and confirmed at the destination location. Null until transfer is fully or partially received.']
  transfer_request_date date [note: 'Date when the stock transfer was initially requested. Used for tracking lead times and transfer cycle analytics.']
  transfer_ship_date date [note: 'Date when the inventory physically left the origin location. Used for in-transit tracking and speed of service metrics.']
  transfer_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the stock transfer: requested (initiated but pending approval), approved (authorized for execution), rejected (denied by approver), in-transit (shipped but not yet received), received (fully received and confirmed), cancelled (voided before completion), partially-received (some items received, others pending). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: requested|approved|rejected|in-transit|received|cancelled|partially-received — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  transfer_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the transfer based on origin and destination: inter-unit (between restaurant units), intra-unit (between storage locations within same unit), return-to-dc (from unit back to distribution center), return-to-vendor (from unit back to supplier), emergency (urgent replenishment), rebalance (inventory optimization transfer).']
  variance_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether a discrepancy was identified between shipped quantities and received quantities. True if any line item shows variance requiring investigation.']
  variance_reason varchar [note: 'Root cause classification for any quantity variance: damage-in-transit (items damaged during shipment), short-shipment (fewer items sent than documented), overage (more items received than expected), quality-rejection (items rejected at receiving), counting-error (human error in count), none (no variance).']

  Note: 'Records the movement of inventory between restaurant units, between storage locations within a unit, or from a unit back to a distribution center — capturing both transfer header and line-level SKU detail in a single consolidated entity. Header attributes: transfer date, transfer type (inter-unit/intra-unit/return-to-vendor/return-to-DC), origin and destination units/locations, transfer status, requested-by/approved-by employees, and total transfer value. Line attributes (one per SKU transferred): stock item reference, transferred quantity, unit of measure, unit cost, extended cost, lot number, expiration date, and condition code. Maintains inventory accuracy across the restaurant network and supports inter-unit food cost allocation.'
}

Table inventory.uom {
  uom_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the unit of measure. Primary key.']
  base_uom_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the base UOM for this measurement type. Used to establish conversion chains. Null if this UOM is itself the base unit.']
  abbreviation varchar [note: 'Short form or symbol for the UOM (e.g., lb, oz, gal, kg, g, ml). Used in compact displays, labels, and Kitchen Display System (KDS) screens.']
  allows_fractional_quantities boolean [note: 'Indicates whether fractional quantities are permitted for this UOM (e.g., 2.5 pounds allowed, but 2.5 cases may not be). Enforces business rules in inventory transactions and recipe scaling.']
  allows_temperature_tracking boolean [note: 'Indicates whether items measured in this UOM require Hazard Analysis Critical Control Points (HACCP) temperature monitoring during storage and transfer. Supports food safety compliance per Food and Drug Administration (FDA) regulations.']
  applicable_item_categories varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of Stock Keeping Unit (SKU) categories or commodity types where this UOM is applicable (e.g., dry goods, produce, dairy, beverages, proteins). Supports context-aware UOM selection in ordering and recipe management.']
  category varchar [note: 'Measurement system category (metric, imperial, count-based, or custom). Supports multi-region operations with different measurement standards.']
  code varchar [note: 'Short alphanumeric code representing the unit of measure (e.g., EA, CS, LB, OZ, GAL, KG, G, ML, L). Used as the business identifier in inventory transactions and recipe management.']
  conversion_factor_to_base decimal(18,6) [note: 'Multiplier to convert this UOM to the base UOM (e.g., 1 pound = 453.592 grams, so conversion_factor_to_base = 453.592). Critical for accurate Cost of Goods Sold (COGS%) calculation and yield management across ordering, storage, and recipe contexts.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this UOM record was first created in the system. Supports audit trails and data lineage tracking per Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (GAAP) requirements.']
  default_shelf_life_days varchar [note: 'Default shelf life in days for items measured in this UOM. Used as a fallback when Stock Keeping Unit (SKU)-specific shelf life is not defined. Supports waste tracking (Waste%) and First In First Out (FIFO) rotation.']
  display_sequence varchar [note: 'Sort order for displaying UOMs in user interfaces and dropdown lists. Lower numbers appear first. Supports user experience optimization in Point of Sale (POS) and inventory management systems.']
  effective_end_date date [note: 'Date when this UOM was retired or deprecated. Null for currently active UOMs. Supports historical reporting and audit trails for Cost of Goods Sold (COGS%) analysis.']
  effective_start_date date [note: 'Date when this UOM became available for use in inventory transactions. Supports temporal validity and historical analysis of UOM changes.']
  is_base_uom boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this UOM is the base unit for its type (e.g., gram for weight, milliliter for volume). Base UOMs have a conversion factor of 1.0 and serve as the reference for all conversions within their type.']
  is_system_standard boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this UOM is a system-defined standard (e.g., gram, liter, each) that cannot be modified or deleted by users. Protects core reference data integrity.']
  iso_code varchar [note: 'ISO 80000 standard code for the unit of measure. Supports international operations and regulatory compliance across multiple jurisdictions.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this UOM record was last updated. Supports change tracking and data governance for Cost of Goods Sold (COGS%) accuracy and regulatory compliance.']
  name varchar [note: 'Full descriptive name of the unit of measure (e.g., Each, Case, Pound, Ounce, Gallon, Kilogram, Gram, Milliliter, Liter). Human-readable label for reporting and user interfaces.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional context, usage guidelines, or special handling instructions for this UOM. Supports operational documentation and training materials.']
  ordering_uom_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this UOM is used in purchase orders and vendor ordering (e.g., case, pallet). Supports procurement workflows and vendor catalog integration.']
  plural_name varchar [note: 'Plural form of the UOM name (e.g., Pounds, Ounces, Cases). Supports grammatically correct reporting and user interface text generation.']
  precision_decimal_places varchar [note: 'Number of decimal places to use when displaying or calculating quantities in this UOM. Ensures consistent rounding and precision across inventory transactions.']
  recipe_uom_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this UOM is used in recipe Bill of Materials (BOM) and menu item costing (e.g., ounce, gram, tablespoon). Critical for accurate recipe yield management and menu engineering.']
  requires_lot_tracking boolean [note: 'Indicates whether items measured in this UOM require lot number and expiration date tracking for traceability. Supports food safety recalls and Good Manufacturing Practice (GMP) compliance.']
  storage_uom_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this UOM is used for Back of House (BOH) inventory storage and stock tracking (e.g., each, bag, box). Supports Periodic Automatic Replenishment (PAR) level management and cycle counts.']
  symbol varchar [note: 'Standard international symbol for the UOM (e.g., °F, °C, %, #). Used in scientific and regulatory contexts, particularly for Hazard Analysis Critical Control Points (HACCP) temperature monitoring.']
  un_cefact_code varchar [note: 'Standardized UN/CEFACT Recommendation 20 code for the unit of measure. Supports Electronic Data Interchange (EDI) integration with suppliers and third-party logistics providers.']
  uom_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the UOM. Active UOMs are available for use in transactions; deprecated UOMs are retained for historical data but not available for new transactions.']
  uom_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the unit of measure by dimension type. Determines conversion logic and applicability to different inventory contexts (receiving, storage, recipe). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: weight|volume|count|length|area|temperature|time — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']

  Note: 'Reference master for all units of measure used in inventory transactions — ordering UOM, storage UOM, and recipe UOM — plus their conversion factors. Captures UOM code, UOM name, UOM type (weight/volume/count/length), base UOM flag, and applicable item categories. Conversion detail: from-UOM, to-UOM, stock-item-specific overrides (item-specific conversions override global defaults), conversion factor, effective date, and source (vendor spec/lab measurement/standard). Supports accurate translation between ordering (case of 6), storage (each), and recipe (ounce/gram) contexts — critical for inventory valuation and COGS% calculation across all restaurant units.'
}

Table restaurant.unit {
  unit_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the restaurant unit. Primary key for the unit master record.']
  brand_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to restaurant.brand. Business justification: Unit should reference its brand via a foreign key rather than a free‑text code; brand_id provides referential integrity.']
  brand_standard_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to restaurant.brand_standard. Business justification: Units must be linked to the applicable brand standard for compliance tracking.']
  department_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.department. Business justification: Linking a unit to its department enables organizational reporting and eliminates the department table silo.']
  address_line1 varchar [note: 'Primary street address of the restaurant unit including street number and name.']
  address_line2 varchar [note: 'Secondary address information such as suite number, building name, or floor.']
  average_cover_count varchar [note: 'Average number of guests (covers) served per day. Used for labor planning and revenue forecasting in table-service restaurants.']
  average_ticket_time_seconds varchar [note: 'Average time in seconds for a customer order to be completed from entry to fulfillment, tracked via KDS (Kitchen Display System).']
  average_unit_volume_usd decimal(15,2) [note: 'Average annual sales revenue for this unit in US dollars. Key performance metric for franchise valuation and benchmarking.']
  city varchar [note: 'City or municipality where the restaurant unit is located.']
  closure_date date [note: 'The date this restaurant unit permanently ceased operations. Null for active units.']
  concept_type varchar [note: 'The restaurant format classification. QSR (Quick-Service Restaurant) for fast food, casual dining for table service, fast casual for counter service with higher quality, fine dining for upscale full service, food court for mall locations, ghost kitchen for delivery-only.']
  country_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO country code where the restaurant unit is located.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this unit record was first created in the system.']
  daypart_schedule varchar [note: 'Structured schedule defining daypart time windows (breakfast, lunch, dinner, late night) for menu and pricing management.']
  drive_thru_lanes varchar [note: 'Number of drive-thru lanes available at this unit. Zero indicates no drive-thru capability.']
  email_address varchar [note: 'Primary email address for the restaurant unit used for operational communications.']
  haccp_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this unit maintains current HACCP certification for food safety management.']
  has_online_ordering boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this unit supports online ordering through web or mobile app channels.']
  has_third_party_delivery boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this unit is enabled for third-party delivery services such as DoorDash, Uber Eats, or Grubhub.']
  health_inspection_score varchar [note: 'Most recent health inspection score from local health department. Scale and passing thresholds vary by jurisdiction.']
  kds_station_count varchar [note: 'Number of KDS stations installed in the BOH for order routing and kitchen workflow management.']
  last_inspection_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent health and safety inspection by local regulatory authority.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this unit record was most recently updated.']
  latitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic latitude coordinate of the restaurant unit in decimal degrees.']
  longitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic longitude coordinate of the restaurant unit in decimal degrees.']
  opening_date date [note: 'The date this restaurant unit first opened for business to customers.']
  operating_hours varchar [note: 'Standard operating hours for the unit, typically formatted as day ranges with time windows (e.g., Mon-Fri 6:00-22:00, Sat-Sun 7:00-23:00).']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the restaurant unit. Active indicates normal operations, temporarily closed for renovations or seasonal closure, permanently closed for shut down units, under construction for new builds, pending opening for units awaiting launch.']
  ownership_model varchar [note: 'The operating model for this unit: company-owned (corporate operated), franchised (independent franchisee), joint venture (shared ownership), or licensed (brand licensing agreement).']
  parking_spaces varchar [note: 'Number of dedicated parking spaces available for guest use at this location.']
  phone_number varchar [note: 'Primary contact phone number for the restaurant unit.']
  pos_system_version varchar [note: 'Version identifier of the POS system software deployed at this unit.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code for the restaurant unit location.']
  same_store_sales_pct decimal(5,2) [note: 'Year-over-year comparable store sales growth percentage. Measures organic growth for units open at least 12 months.']
  seating_capacity varchar [note: 'Maximum number of guest seats available in the FOH (Front of House) dining area.']
  speed_of_service_seconds varchar [note: 'Average time in seconds from order placement to order delivery. Critical QSR operational metric for customer satisfaction.']
  square_footage varchar [note: 'Total interior floor space of the restaurant unit in square feet, including FOH and BOH (Back of House) areas.']
  state_province varchar [note: 'Two-letter state or province code where the restaurant unit is located.']
  table_turn_rate decimal(4,2) [note: 'Average number of times a table is occupied by different parties during a service period. Relevant for casual and fine-dining concepts.']
  throughput_capacity_orders_per_hour varchar [note: 'Maximum number of customer orders this unit can process per hour at peak efficiency, based on equipment and staffing design.']
  trade_name varchar [note: 'The customer-facing name of the restaurant unit as displayed on signage and marketing materials.']
  unit_number varchar [note: 'Business identifier for the restaurant unit. Externally-known unique code used across operational systems and franchise communications.']

  Note: 'Master record for every restaurant unit — company-owned and franchised. The authoritative identity of each physical location including unit number, brand, concept type (QSR/casual/fine-dining), ownership model (company-owned vs. franchised), legal entity name, trade name, opening date, closure date, current operational status, and geographic coordinates. This is the operational anchor for the entire restaurant domain and the primary FK target for all cross-domain joins (order, inventory, workforce, finance, franchise). One row per physical restaurant location. All other restaurant domain products reference this entity.'
}

Table restaurant.location_profile {
  location_profile_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the restaurant location profile. Primary key for this entity.']
  primary_location_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the master restaurant unit record. Links this location profile to the operational restaurant entity.']
  address_line_1 varchar [note: 'Primary street address of the restaurant location including street number and name. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  address_line_2 varchar [note: 'Secondary address information such as suite, unit, or building number. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  building_ownership_type varchar [note: 'Type of ownership or lease arrangement for the restaurant building. Impacts financial reporting and CapEx decisions.']
  city varchar [note: 'City or municipality where the restaurant is located. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  country_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO country code identifying the country where the restaurant operates.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this location profile record was first created in the system. Audit trail for data lineage.']
  delivery_radius_km decimal(6,2) [note: 'Maximum delivery radius in kilometers for online ordering and third-party delivery services. Configured per location based on geography and capacity.']
  dma_code varchar [note: 'Nielsen Designated Market Area code identifying the media market. Used for marketing campaign planning and regional media analysis.']
  drive_thru_lane_count varchar [note: 'Number of drive-thru lanes at the restaurant. Critical for throughput capacity planning and speed of service benchmarking.']
  email_address varchar [note: 'Primary email address for the restaurant location. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  fax_number varchar [note: 'Fax number for the restaurant location if applicable. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  has_accessible_parking boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has ADA-compliant accessible parking spaces. Required for regulatory compliance.']
  has_drive_thru boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has drive-thru service capability. Key differentiator for QSR format and operational model.']
  has_patio_seating boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has outdoor patio seating available. Impacts cover count and seasonal capacity.']
  has_playground boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has a children\'s playground. Differentiator for family-oriented locations.']
  has_restroom boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has customer restroom facilities. Impacts guest experience and local health code compliance.']
  has_wheelchair_access boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant has wheelchair-accessible entrances and facilities. Required for ADA compliance.']
  has_wifi boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant offers guest WiFi service. Enhances guest experience and supports digital ordering.']
  last_remodel_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent major remodel or renovation. Used for CapEx tracking and brand standard compliance.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this location profile record was last modified. Audit trail for data lineage and change tracking.']
  latitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic latitude coordinate of the restaurant location in decimal degrees. Used for mapping, delivery radius calculation, and proximity analytics.']
  lease_expiration_date date [note: 'Date when the current lease agreement expires. Critical for real estate planning and renewal negotiations.']
  locale varchar [note: 'Locale identifier for language and regional formatting preferences. Used for menu display, signage, and customer communications.']
  longitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic longitude coordinate of the restaurant location in decimal degrees. Used for mapping, delivery radius calculation, and proximity analytics.']
  lot_size_sqft varchar [note: 'Total lot size in square feet including building, parking, and landscaping. Used for site selection and property management.']
  parking_capacity varchar [note: 'Total number of parking spaces available at or near the restaurant location. Influences site selection and accessibility.']
  patio_seat_count varchar [note: 'Number of seats available on the outdoor patio. Used for capacity planning and table turn analysis.']
  phone_number varchar [note: 'Primary contact phone number for the restaurant location. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code for the restaurant location. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  proximity_to_airport_km decimal(8,2) [note: 'Distance in kilometers from the restaurant to the nearest airport. Influences traveler traffic and site classification.']
  proximity_to_highway_km decimal(8,2) [note: 'Distance in kilometers from the restaurant to the nearest major highway. Used for site selection and trade area analysis.']
  proximity_to_mall_km decimal(8,2) [note: 'Distance in kilometers from the restaurant to the nearest shopping mall. Impacts foot traffic and co-location strategy.']
  restaurant_number varchar [note: 'Business identifier for the restaurant unit. Externally-known code used in operations, reporting, and franchise communications.']
  site_closed_date date [note: 'Date when the restaurant location permanently closed. Used for portfolio analysis and historical reporting.']
  site_opened_date date [note: 'Date when the restaurant location first opened for business. Used for NRO tracking, maturity analysis, and comp sales calculations.']
  site_status varchar [note: 'Current operational status of the restaurant site. Used for reporting, site portfolio management, and NRO tracking.']
  square_footage varchar [note: 'Total interior square footage of the restaurant building. Used for real estate valuation, CapEx planning, and operational benchmarking.']
  state_province varchar [note: 'State, province, or administrative region code. Organizational contact data classified as confidential.']
  timezone varchar [note: 'IANA timezone identifier for the restaurant location. Used for daypart scheduling, operating hours, and time-based reporting.']
  trade_area_classification varchar [note: 'Classification of the trade area type where the restaurant is located. Influences site selection, menu offerings, and operational strategies. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: urban|suburban|rural|highway|airport|mall|downtown|neighborhood — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']

  Note: 'Physical and geographic attributes of each restaurant unit including full street address, city, state, province, postal code, country, DMA (Designated Market Area), trade area classification, latitude/longitude, timezone, locale, accessibility features, parking capacity, drive-thru lane count, patio seating availability, and proximity to key landmarks. Supports site analytics, delivery radius configuration, and regional reporting.'
}

Table restaurant.format_config {
  format_config_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the restaurant format configuration record. Primary key.']
  approved_by_employee_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.employee. Business justification: Format configuration changes (service model, seating capacity, drive-thru layout) require management approval. Tracking the approving employee is a real operational audit and brand-standard compliance']
  brand_id varchar [note: 'add column brand_id (BIGINT) with FK to restaurant.brand.brand_id - format configurations (QSR/casual/fine-dining) should be brand-specific']
  brand_standard_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to restaurant.brand_standard. Business justification: A format configuration (QSR, casual, fine-dining) must comply with specific brand standards and SOPs. format_config already tracks brand_standard_compliance_status as a STRING, indicating the relation']
  primary_format_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the restaurant unit to which this format configuration applies.']
  alcohol_service_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format is licensed and configured to serve alcoholic beverages.']
  boh_cooking_line_count varchar [note: 'Number of cooking lines or production stations in the Back of House (BOH) kitchen.']
  boh_kitchen_sq_ft decimal(10,2) [note: 'Total square footage of the Back of House (BOH) kitchen and food preparation area.']
  boh_prep_station_count varchar [note: 'Number of food preparation stations in the Back of House (BOH) kitchen.']
  brand_standard_compliance_status varchar [note: 'Current compliance status of the format configuration against brand standards and Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs).']
  breakfast_daypart_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format operates during the breakfast daypart and serves breakfast menu items.']
  config_version varchar [note: 'Version identifier for this format configuration, used to track changes and updates over time.']
  cover_count_capacity_per_daypart varchar [note: 'Maximum number of covers (individual meals served) the restaurant can accommodate during a single daypart (breakfast, lunch, dinner) based on seating and throughput.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this format configuration record was first created in the system.']
  curbside_pickup_enabled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format supports curbside pickup service for online orders.']
  dining_format_type varchar [note: 'The primary dining format classification of the restaurant unit: Quick-Service Restaurant (QSR), fast-casual, casual dining, or fine-dining.']
  drive_thru_lane_config varchar [note: 'Drive-Thru (DT) lane configuration: none (no drive-thru), single lane, dual lane, or triple lane.']
  drive_thru_window_count varchar [note: 'Number of Drive-Thru (DT) service windows available for order pickup.']
  effective_date date [note: 'Date when this format configuration became or will become effective for the restaurant unit.']
  expiration_date date [note: 'Date when this format configuration expires or is superseded by a new configuration. Null indicates current active configuration.']
  foh_bar_seating_count varchar [note: 'Number of bar seats available in the Front of House (FOH) area, if applicable.']
  foh_outdoor_seating_count varchar [note: 'Number of outdoor patio or sidewalk seats available in the Front of House (FOH) area.']
  foh_seating_capacity varchar [note: 'Total number of guest seats available in the Front of House (FOH) dining area, including indoor and outdoor seating.']
  foh_table_count varchar [note: 'Total number of dining tables in the Front of House (FOH) area.']
  format_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or label for this format configuration (e.g., \'Standard QSR with DT\', \'Urban Fast-Casual\').']
  kds_screen_count varchar [note: 'Number of Kitchen Display System (KDS) screens installed in the Back of House (BOH) for order routing and production management.']
  kiosk_count varchar [note: 'Number of self-service digital ordering kiosks installed in the restaurant.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this format configuration record was last updated or modified.']
  last_remodel_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent remodel or renovation that affected the format configuration of the restaurant.']
  late_night_daypart_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format operates during late-night hours (typically after 10 PM).']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes or comments regarding special considerations, exceptions, or unique characteristics of this format configuration.']
  olo_enabled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format supports Online Ordering (OLO) through web or mobile app channels.']
  parking_space_count varchar [note: 'Number of parking spaces available for guest use at the restaurant location.']
  pos_terminal_count varchar [note: 'Number of Point of Sale (POS) terminals installed for order capture and payment processing.']
  service_model varchar [note: 'Primary service delivery model: counter service, table service, drive-thru, self-service kiosk, or hybrid combination.']
  sos_benchmark_seconds varchar [note: 'Target Speed of Service (SOS) benchmark in seconds from order placement to order delivery, based on format and service model.']
  table_turn_target_minutes varchar [note: 'Target table turn time in minutes for table-service formats, representing the average time from guest seating to table availability.']
  third_party_delivery_enabled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant format supports Third-Party Delivery (3PD) integration with external delivery platforms.']
  throughput_capacity_per_hour varchar [note: 'Maximum number of customer transactions the restaurant can process per hour under optimal conditions, based on format configuration.']
  twenty_four_hour_operation_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant operates 24 hours per day, 7 days per week.']

  Note: 'Defines the operational format, physical configuration, and total capacity of a restaurant unit. Includes dining format (QSR, fast-casual, casual, fine-dining), service model (counter, table, drive-thru, kiosk), FOH layout (total indoor seating capacity, outdoor/patio seating capacity, bar seating count, private dining room capacity, ADA-compliant seating count, table count, cover count), BOH layout (kitchen footprint sq ft, cooking lines, prep stations), drive-thru configuration (lane count, stacking capacity for vehicles), kiosk count, counter service positions, and maximum cover count per daypart. This is the single source of truth for all physical capacity and layout attributes of a unit — governs brand standard compliance, throughput benchmarking, labor staffing ratios, health department permit compliance, and fire marshal occupancy limits.'
}

Table restaurant.operating_hours {
  operating_hours_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the operating hours record. Primary key.']
  primary_operating_approved_by_user_employee_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user who approved this operating hours schedule. Used for audit trail and compliance tracking.']
  primary_operating_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the restaurant unit to which these operating hours apply. Links to the restaurant master record.']
  approved_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when this operating hours schedule was approved, in yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format. Used for audit trail and compliance tracking.']
  close_time timestamp [note: 'The time the restaurant closes for the specified day and daypart, in 24-hour HH:mm format (e.g., 22:00). May extend past midnight for late-night operations.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when this operating hours record was first created in the system, in yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format. Used for audit trail and data lineage.']
  day_of_week varchar [note: 'The day of the week for which these operating hours are defined. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  daypart varchar [note: 'The daypart segment (breakfast, lunch, dinner, late-night, all-day, overnight) for which these hours apply. Used for menu availability, labor scheduling, and Same-Store Sales (SSS) period alignment.']
  daypart_end_time timestamp [note: 'The end time of the specific daypart window (e.g., breakfast ends at 11:00), in 24-hour HH:mm format. Used for menu engineering and Product Mix (PMIX) analysis.']
  daypart_start_time timestamp [note: 'The start time of the specific daypart window (e.g., breakfast starts at 06:00), in 24-hour HH:mm format. Used for menu engineering and Product Mix (PMIX) analysis.']
  delivery_window_close_time timestamp [note: 'The time the restaurant stops accepting delivery orders (Online Ordering (OLO) and Third-Party Delivery (3PD)), in 24-hour HH:mm format. May differ from dine-in hours.']
  delivery_window_open_time timestamp [note: 'The time the restaurant begins accepting delivery orders (Online Ordering (OLO) and Third-Party Delivery (3PD)), in 24-hour HH:mm format. May differ from dine-in hours.']
  drive_thru_close_time timestamp [note: 'The time the Drive-Thru (DT) lane closes, in 24-hour HH:mm format. May differ from Front of House (FOH) close time for QSR (Quick-Service Restaurant) units.']
  drive_thru_open_time timestamp [note: 'The time the Drive-Thru (DT) lane opens, in 24-hour HH:mm format. May differ from Front of House (FOH) open time for QSR (Quick-Service Restaurant) units.']
  effective_end_date date [note: 'The date on which these operating hours expire, in yyyy-MM-dd format. Null for open-ended schedules. Used for temporary hour changes and seasonal adjustments.']
  effective_start_date date [note: 'The date from which these operating hours become effective, in yyyy-MM-dd format. Used for scheduling future hour changes and seasonal adjustments.']
  expected_cover_count varchar [note: 'The expected number of covers (individual diners served) during this daypart for casual and fine-dining establishments. Used for labor scheduling and inventory planning.']
  expected_table_turn_count decimal(5,2) [note: 'The expected number of table turns (times a table is occupied and vacated) during this daypart for casual and fine-dining establishments. Used for capacity planning and revenue forecasting.']
  holiday_name varchar [note: 'The name of the holiday for which this schedule override applies (e.g., Christmas, Thanksgiving, New Year\'s Day). Null if not a holiday override.']
  holiday_schedule_override_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether these operating hours represent a holiday schedule override. True if this is a holiday exception, False for regular schedule.']
  is_24_hour_operation boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant operates 24 hours a day for the specified day. True if open continuously, False otherwise.']
  is_closed boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the restaurant is closed for the specified day and daypart. True if closed, False if open.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The date and time when this operating hours record was last modified, in yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format. Used for audit trail and change tracking.']
  last_order_cutoff_time timestamp [note: 'The time by which the last order must be placed before the restaurant stops accepting new orders, in 24-hour HH:mm format. Used for Online Ordering (OLO) and Third-Party Delivery (3PD) routing.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes or comments about this operating hours schedule. May include reasons for schedule changes, special instructions, or operational context.']
  open_time timestamp [note: 'The time the restaurant opens for the specified day and daypart, in 24-hour HH:mm format (e.g., 06:00).']
  schedule_status varchar [note: 'The current lifecycle status of this operating hours record. Active schedules are in effect; pending schedules are future-dated; expired schedules are past their end date; suspended schedules are temporarily disabled.']
  schedule_type varchar [note: 'The type of operating hours schedule. Regular for standard weekly hours, holiday for holiday overrides, seasonal for seasonal adjustments, temporary for one-time changes, special_event for event-driven hours.']
  seasonal_adjustment_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether these operating hours represent a seasonal adjustment (e.g., extended summer hours, reduced winter hours). True if seasonal, False for standard schedule.']
  seasonal_period_name varchar [note: 'The name of the seasonal period for which this schedule adjustment applies (e.g., Summer, Winter, Back-to-School). Null if not a seasonal adjustment.']
  special_event_name varchar [note: 'The name of the special event for which these operating hours apply (e.g., Super Bowl Sunday, Local Festival, Grand Opening). Null if not a special event schedule.']
  target_speed_of_service_seconds varchar [note: 'The target Speed of Service (SOS) in seconds for this daypart. Represents the benchmark time from order placement to order fulfillment. Used for operational performance tracking.']
  target_ticket_time_seconds varchar [note: 'The target ticket time in seconds for this daypart. Represents the benchmark time from order entry to Kitchen Display System (KDS) completion. Used for Back of House (BOH) performance tracking.']
  throughput_capacity_per_hour varchar [note: 'The estimated number of customer transactions the restaurant can process per hour during this daypart. Used for labor scheduling and Speed of Service (SOS) benchmarking.']

  Note: 'Scheduled operating hours for each restaurant unit by day of week and daypart (breakfast, lunch, dinner, late-night, 24hr). Captures open time, close time, daypart start/end times, holiday schedule overrides, seasonal hour adjustments, drive-thru-specific hours, delivery window hours, and last-order cutoff times. Used for order routing, labor scheduling, and SSS (Same-Store Sales) period alignment.'
}

Table restaurant.equipment_asset {

  department_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.department. Business justification: Equipment assets are owned and maintained by a department (e.g., kitchen equipment under BOH department). Maintenance budgets, depreciation allocation, and HACCP compliance audits are tracked by depar']
  employee_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.employee. Business justification: Asset depreciation and financial reporting require each physical equipment asset to be tied to a fixed‑asset record in finance.']
  kitchen_station_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to restaurant.kitchen_station. Business justification: Equipment assets are physically installed at specific kitchen stations (e.g., fryers at the fry station, grills at the grill station). equipment_asset currently tracks location_zone as a free-text STR']
  primary_equipment_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the restaurant unit where this equipment asset is installed.']
  acquisition_cost_usd decimal(15,2) [note: 'Original purchase price paid for the equipment asset in US dollars, used for CapEx (Capital Expenditure) tracking and depreciation calculations.']
  asset_condition_rating varchar [note: 'Current physical and operational condition assessment of the equipment asset based on inspection and performance criteria.']
  asset_tag_number varchar [note: 'Externally visible unique asset tag or barcode identifier affixed to the equipment for tracking and inventory purposes.']
  compliance_certification_expiry_date date [note: 'Date when the current compliance certification expires and recertification or inspection is required.']
  compliance_certification_number varchar [note: 'Certification or approval number from regulatory bodies (FDA, NSF, UL, OSHA) confirming the equipment meets safety and sanitation standards.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this equipment asset record was first created in the system.']
  depreciation_method varchar [note: 'Accounting method used to allocate the cost of the equipment asset over its useful life for financial reporting and tax purposes.']
  disposal_date date [note: 'Date when the equipment asset was decommissioned and removed from the restaurant, marking the end of its operational lifecycle.']
  disposal_method varchar [note: 'Method by which the equipment was disposed of at end of life, supporting asset lifecycle tracking and environmental compliance.']
  energy_rating varchar [note: 'Energy efficiency rating or certification (e.g., Energy Star rating) indicating the equipment\'s power consumption performance.']
  equipment_category varchar [note: 'High-level classification grouping equipment by operational domain (cooking, refrigeration, POS systems, etc.). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: cooking|refrigeration|food_prep|beverage|pos_system|kitchen_display|sanitation|hvac — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']

[ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: fryer|grill|oven|refrigeration_unit|freezer|ice_machine|pos_terminal|kds_display|espresso_machine|beverage_dispenser|dishwasher|ventilation_hood|warming_cabinet|prep_table — 14 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  installation_date date [note: 'Date when the equipment asset was installed and commissioned at the restaurant location.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this equipment asset record was most recently updated.']
  last_service_date date [note: 'Most recent date when preventive maintenance or repair service was performed on this equipment asset.']
  last_temperature_check_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Most recent date and time when temperature was verified for temperature-critical equipment, supporting food safety audit trails.']
  lease_expiry_date date [note: 'Date when the lease or rental agreement for this equipment expires, requiring renewal or return of the asset.']
  location_zone varchar [note: 'Physical zone within the restaurant where the equipment is installed: BOH (Back of House), FOH (Front of House), Drive-Thru, Storage, or Outdoor.']
  maintenance_frequency_days varchar [note: 'Number of days between scheduled preventive maintenance services as defined by manufacturer guidelines or internal SOPs.']
  manufacturer_name varchar [note: 'Name of the company that manufactured the equipment asset.']
  model_number varchar [note: 'Manufacturer-assigned model number or designation for the equipment.']
  next_scheduled_maintenance_date date [note: 'Planned date for the next preventive maintenance service visit based on manufacturer recommendations and SOP (Standard Operating Procedure) schedules.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional comments, special handling instructions, or historical context about the equipment asset.']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status indicating whether the equipment is actively in use, under maintenance, or retired from service.']
  ownership_type varchar [note: 'Indicates whether the equipment is owned outright by the restaurant, leased under a capital or operating lease, or rented short-term.']
  power_consumption_watts varchar [note: 'Rated electrical power consumption of the equipment in watts under normal operating conditions, used for energy cost forecasting.']
  replacement_cost_usd decimal(15,2) [note: 'Estimated current market cost to replace this equipment asset with a comparable new unit, used for R&M (Repairs and Maintenance) budgeting and insurance valuation.']
  serial_number varchar [note: 'Unique serial number assigned by the manufacturer to this specific equipment unit for warranty and service tracking.']
  service_contract_number varchar [note: 'Reference number of the active service or maintenance contract covering this equipment asset, if applicable.']
  temperature_critical_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this equipment maintains temperature-sensitive food products and requires continuous monitoring for HACCP (Hazard Analysis Critical Control Points) compliance.']
  temperature_max_f decimal(5,2) [note: 'Maximum safe operating temperature threshold in Fahrenheit for temperature-critical equipment, above which food safety is compromised.']
  temperature_min_f decimal(5,2) [note: 'Minimum safe operating temperature threshold in Fahrenheit for temperature-critical equipment, below which food safety is compromised.']
  useful_life_years varchar [note: 'Expected operational lifespan of the equipment asset in years, used for depreciation calculations and replacement planning.']
  warranty_expiry_date date [note: 'Date when the manufacturer or vendor warranty coverage ends, after which repairs and parts are at the restaurant\'s expense.']
  warranty_start_date date [note: 'Date when the manufacturer or vendor warranty coverage begins for this equipment asset.']

  Note: 'Inventory of all BOH and FOH equipment assets installed at each restaurant unit including all equipment types (fryer, grill, oven, KDS stations, POS terminals, refrigeration units, ice machines, espresso machines, drive-thru timers). Captures make, model, serial number, installation date, warranty expiry, last service date, next scheduled maintenance date, asset condition rating, replacement cost, software version (for digital equipment), and equipment-specific configuration attributes. Supports R&M (Repairs and Maintenance) planning, CapEx forecasting, PCI DSS compliance for payment terminals, and food safety compliance for temperature-critical equipment.'
}

Table restaurant.brand_standard {
  brand_standard_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the brand standard record. Primary key.']
  brand_id varchar [note: 'add column brand_id (BIGINT) with FK to restaurant.brand.brand_id - brand standards must explicitly reference which brand they belong to']
  superseded_by_standard_brand_standard_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the brand_standard_id of the newer standard that replaces this one. Null if this standard is current and has not been superseded.']
  applicable_format varchar [note: 'Restaurant format(s) to which this brand standard applies. QSR (Quick-Service Restaurant) for fast food, casual for casual dining, fine_dining for upscale establishments, or combinations/all formats. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: all|qsr|casual|fine_dining|qsr_casual|qsr_fine_dining|casual_fine_dining — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  applicable_ownership_model varchar [note: 'Ownership model(s) to which this standard applies: company-owned units, franchised units, or all units regardless of ownership.']
  audit_frequency varchar [note: 'Expected frequency at which compliance with this standard should be audited or inspected (e.g., daily temperature logs, monthly cleanliness audits, annual safety inspections). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: daily|weekly|monthly|quarterly|semi_annual|annual|ad_hoc — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  brand_standard_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the brand standard. Active standards are in force. Inactive standards are no longer enforced. Draft standards are under development. Under_review standards are being revised. Superseded standards have been replaced by a newer version.']
  certification_body varchar [note: 'Name of the external organization or authority that issues the required certification (e.g., National Restaurant Association ServSafe, OSHA, Local Health Department). Null if no certification is required.']
  certification_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether staff must obtain a formal certification to perform tasks related to this standard (e.g., ServSafe Food Handler certification for food safety standards). True if certification is mandatory, False otherwise.']
  compliance_requirement_level varchar [note: 'Enforcement level of the standard. Mandatory standards must be met for operational compliance and are subject to audit consequences. Recommended standards are best practices encouraged but not enforced. Aspirational standards represent future-state goals.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this brand standard record was first created in the system.']
  effective_date date [note: 'Date on which this brand standard becomes active and enforceable. Standards are not applicable before this date.']
  expiry_date date [note: 'Date on which this brand standard is no longer active or enforceable. Null indicates an open-ended standard with no planned expiration.']
  governing_body_reference varchar [note: 'External regulatory or industry body that mandates or influences this standard (e.g., FDA, USDA, OSHA, NRA ServSafe, Local Health Department, ISO 22000). Null if the standard is purely internal.']
  guest_facing_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this standard directly impacts the guest experience and is visible or perceptible to customers (e.g., FOH cleanliness, service speed, food presentation). True if guest-facing, False if back-of-house or internal only.']
  last_review_date date [note: 'Date on which this brand standard was last reviewed for accuracy, relevance, and compliance with current regulations. Used to track review cycles and ensure standards remain current.']
  measurement_method varchar [note: 'Method by which compliance with this standard is assessed: direct observation (visual inspection), checklist (structured audit form), measurement (thermometer, timer), testing (lab analysis, swab test), documentation review (log verification), or guest feedback (CSAT, NPS).']
  next_review_date date [note: 'Scheduled date for the next formal review of this brand standard. Ensures periodic reassessment to maintain relevance and regulatory alignment.']
  non_compliance_consequence varchar [note: 'Consequence or remediation action triggered when a unit fails to meet this standard. Ranges from warnings and corrective action plans to financial penalties, operational suspension, or franchise termination for critical violations.']
  owner_department varchar [note: 'Internal department or functional area responsible for defining, maintaining, and enforcing this brand standard (e.g., Food Safety, Operations, Quality Assurance, Franchise Development, Marketing).']
  priority_level varchar [note: 'Business priority level of the standard. Critical standards directly impact guest safety or regulatory compliance. High standards affect brand reputation or operational efficiency. Medium and low standards are important but less urgent.']
  sop_document_reference varchar [note: 'Reference identifier or URI to the detailed SOP document that operationalizes this brand standard. May be a document management system ID, SharePoint link, or file path.']
  standard_category varchar [note: 'High-level classification of the brand standard into operational domains: food quality (taste, temperature, freshness), food safety (HACCP compliance, contamination prevention), cleanliness (sanitation, hygiene), service excellence (guest interaction, speed of service), brand presentation (signage, uniforms, decor), workplace safety (OSHA compliance, injury prevention), or equipment maintenance (preventive maintenance, calibration). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: food_quality|food_safety|cleanliness|service_excellence|brand_presentation|workplace_safety|equipment_maintenance — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  standard_code varchar [note: 'Unique business identifier code for the brand standard, typically following a category-number pattern (e.g., FS-001 for Food Safety standard 001, CL-042 for Cleanliness standard 042).']
  standard_description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative description of the brand standard, including the specific requirement, rationale, and expected outcome. May reference specific procedures, thresholds, or quality criteria.']
  standard_name varchar [note: 'Full descriptive name of the brand standard (e.g., \'Proper Handwashing Protocol\', \'Drive-Thru Speed of Service Target\', \'Uniform and Appearance Guidelines\').']
  target_metric_unit varchar [note: 'Unit of measure for the target metric value (e.g., degrees Fahrenheit, seconds, percentage, count). Null if the standard does not have a quantitative metric.']
  target_metric_value decimal(18,2) [note: 'Specific quantitative or qualitative target that defines compliance with this standard (e.g., \'Food temperature >= 165°F\', \'Speed of Service <= 90 seconds\', \'Cleanliness score >= 95%\', \'Zero critical violations\'). Null if the standard is qualitative without a numeric threshold.']
  training_module_reference varchar [note: 'Reference identifier to the training module or course that covers this brand standard. May be an LMS (Learning Management System) course ID or training document reference. Null if no formal training module exists.']
  training_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether formal training is required for staff to understand and comply with this standard. True if training is mandatory, False otherwise.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this brand standard record was last modified in the system.']
  version_number varchar [note: 'Version identifier for the standard, following semantic versioning (e.g., 1.0, 2.1, 3.0). Incremented when the standard is revised or updated.']

  Note: 'Defines the brand standards and SOPs (Standard Operating Procedures) applicable to each restaurant unit by concept type and ownership model. Captures standard code, standard name, standard category (food quality, cleanliness, service, safety, brand presentation), applicable format (QSR/casual/fine-dining), compliance requirement level (mandatory/recommended), effective date, expiry date, governing body reference (NRA, FDA, OSHA), and linked SOP document reference. Operational anchor for audit and compliance workflows.'
}

Table restaurant.kitchen_station {
  kitchen_station_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for kitchen_station']
  department_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.department. Business justification: Kitchen stations belong to a department (BOH prep, grill, expo). Workforce scheduling, labor cost allocation, and compliance audits are reported by department. Linking kitchen_station to department en']
  employee_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the employee responsible for the station.']
  location_profile_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the restaurant location where the station resides.']
  unit_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to restaurant.unit. Business justification: Kitchen stations belong to a specific restaurant unit; adding unit_id creates the necessary parent link and eliminates isolation.']
  upstream_kitchen_station_id varchar [note: 'Self-referencing FK on kitchen_station (upstream_kitchen_station_id)']
  area_sqft decimal(8,2) [note: 'Physical floor area occupied by the station.']
  average_ticket_time_seconds varchar [note: 'Average time a ticket spends at this station.']
  capacity_dishes varchar [note: 'Maximum number of dishes the station can handle concurrently.']
  daypart_schedule varchar [note: 'Meal periods during which the station is active.']
  effective_from date [note: 'Date when the station became operational.']
  effective_until date [note: 'Date when the station is scheduled to be retired or decommissioned (null if open‑ended).']
  equipment_list varchar [note: 'Comma‑separated list of major equipment assigned to the station.']
  health_inspection_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent health inspection.']
  health_inspection_status varchar [note: 'Result of the most recent health inspection for the station.']
  is_automated boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the station includes automated equipment.']
  kitchen_station_status varchar [note: 'Current operational status of the station.']
  last_maintenance_date date [note: 'Date when the station last underwent preventive maintenance.']
  maintenance_interval_days varchar [note: 'Scheduled number of days between routine maintenance events.']
  operational_hours varchar [note: 'Standard daily operating window (e.g., "08:00-22:00").']
  power_rating_kw decimal(10,2) [note: 'Maximum electrical power consumption of the station.']
  record_audit_created timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the kitchen station record was first created.']
  record_audit_updated timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the kitchen station record.']
  speed_of_service_sos_seconds varchar [note: 'Target time in seconds to complete a dish at this station.']
  station_code varchar [note: 'Business identifier code used in operational systems and SOPs.']
  station_name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the kitchen station (e.g., "Grill Station 1").']
  station_type varchar [note: 'Category of the station based on function (e.g., prep, cook, plating).']
  temperature_control boolean [note: 'Indicates if the station has temperature‑control capabilities.']
  temperature_range_c varchar [note: 'Allowed temperature range for the station (e.g., "0-5").']
  throughput_per_hour varchar [note: 'Average number of dishes processed by the station each hour.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for kitchen_station. Referenced by station_id.'
}

Table restaurant.brand {
  brand_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for brand']
  parent_brand_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the parent brand in a brand hierarchy, if applicable.']

  average_daily_customers varchar [note: 'Mean number of guests served per day across brand locations.']
  average_employee_count varchar [note: 'Typical number of staff employed per restaurant for the brand.']
  average_store_size_sqft decimal(10,2) [note: 'Mean square‑footage of restaurants operating under the brand.']
  brand_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle state of the brand.']
  brand_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the brand by service format.']
  category varchar [note: 'High‑level category describing the brand\'s product focus.']
  code varchar [note: 'External alphanumeric code used to reference the brand in corporate systems.']
  color_hex varchar [note: 'Hexadecimal representation of the brand\'s primary color.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date‑time when the brand record was first created in the system.']
  description varchar [note: 'Narrative description of the brand\'s positioning and concept.']
  established_date date [note: 'Date the brand was first launched.']
  franchise_allowed boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the brand is offered as a franchise model.']
  franchise_fee_percent decimal(5,2) [note: 'Initial franchise fee expressed as a percent of initial investment.']
  headquarters_city varchar [note: 'City where the brand\'s corporate headquarters is located.']

  logo_url varchar [note: 'Link to the brand\'s primary logo image.']

  name varchar [note: 'Human‑readable name of the brand.']
  primary_market_region varchar [note: 'Geographic region where the brand has its strongest presence.']
  royalty_fee_percent decimal(5,2) [note: 'Ongoing royalty fee charged to franchisees, expressed as a percent of sales.']
  segment varchar [note: 'Target consumer segment for the brand.']
  social_media_handle varchar [note: 'Primary social media identifier (e.g., @brand) used for marketing.']
  sos_target_seconds varchar [note: 'Targeted service time from order to delivery for the brand.']
  tagline varchar [note: 'Short marketing slogan associated with the brand.']
  updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date‑time of the most recent modification to the brand record.']
  website_url varchar [note: 'Public website address for the brand.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for brand. Referenced by brand_id.'
}

Table workforce.employee {
  employee_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique system-generated identifier for each employee.']
  department_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.department. Business justification: The employee table currently stores department as a free-text STRING column, which is a denormalization anti-pattern. The workforce domain has a dedicated department master table with department_id as']
  employee_restaurant_unit_id varchar [note: 'Primary restaurant location to which the employee is assigned.']
  employee_unit_id varchar [note: 'Primary restaurant location to which the employee is assigned.']
  employee_work_location_unit_id varchar [note: 'Specific work site (e.g., kitchen, dining area) within the restaurant.']
  manager_employee_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the employee\'s direct manager.']
  position_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to workforce.position. Business justification: An employee holds a specific authorized position within the restaurant organization. This FK normalizes the employee-to-position relationship, enabling direct joins for role-based reporting, schedulin']
  address_line1 varchar [note: 'Primary street address of the employee\'s residence.']
  address_line2 varchar [note: 'Secondary address information (apartment, suite, etc.).']
  bank_account_number varchar [note: 'Bank account number used for direct deposit of payroll.']
  city varchar [note: 'City of the employee\'s residence.']
  country varchar [note: 'Three‑letter ISO country code of the employee\'s residence. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: USA|CAN|MEX|GBR|FRA|DEU|JPN|CHN — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  date_of_birth date [note: 'Birth date of the employee, used for age‑based compliance and benefits.']
  email varchar [note: 'Primary email address used for employee communications.']
  emergency_contact_name varchar [note: 'Name of the person to contact in case of emergency.']
  emergency_contact_phone varchar [note: 'Phone number of the emergency contact.']
  employment_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the employee within the organization.']
  employment_type varchar [note: 'Classification of employment relationship (Full‑Time, Part‑Time, Contractor).']
  first_name varchar [note: 'Legal first name of the employee.']
  full_name varchar [note: 'Concatenated first and last name for display purposes.']
  hire_date date [note: 'Date the employee started employment with the company.']
  labor_percentage_target decimal(5,2) [note: 'Target labor cost as a percentage of sales for the employee\'s role.']
  last_name varchar [note: 'Legal last name of the employee.']
  overtime_eligible boolean [note: 'Indicates if the employee is eligible for overtime pay.']
  pay_grade varchar [note: 'Internal pay grade code used for compensation planning.']
  phone_number varchar [note: 'Primary contact phone number for the employee.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code of the employee\'s residence.']
  record_audit_created timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the employee record was first created in the system.']
  record_audit_updated timestamp [note: 'Timestamp of the most recent update to the employee record.']
  role_classification varchar [note: 'Primary functional area where the employee works: Back‑of‑House, Front‑of‑House, Administrative, Support.']
  salary_amount decimal(12,2) [note: 'Annual base salary amount before bonuses or overtime.']
  salary_currency varchar [note: 'Three‑letter ISO currency code for the salary amount.']
  servsafe_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the employee holds a valid ServSafe food safety certification.']
  servsafe_expiration_date date [note: 'Expiration date of the employee\'s ServSafe certification.']
  shift_pattern varchar [note: 'Standard shift pattern assigned to the employee.']
  state varchar [note: 'State or province of the employee\'s residence.']
  tax_identifier varchar [note: 'Government‑issued tax identification number (e.g., SSN, SIN).']
  termination_date date [note: 'Date the employee\'s employment ended, if applicable.']
  union_member boolean [note: 'True if the employee is a member of a labor union.']
  work_schedule_type varchar [note: 'Scheduling model applied to the employee.']

  Note: 'Master record for every restaurant employee across company-owned and franchised locations. Captures full employee lifecycle data including personal details, employment type (FTE/PTE), BOH/FOH role classification, hire date, termination date, employment status, pay grade, home restaurant assignment, Workday HCM employee ID, declared availability windows (preferred dayparts, max weekly hours, blackout dates, cross-location availability), and current benefit enrollment summary. Single source of truth for workforce identity and worker profile across the enterprise.'
}

Table workforce.position {
  position_id varchar [pk, note: 'System-generated unique identifier for the job position.']
